# Awesome Ads

Stand: 2026-11-12. 545 Einträge. Auswahlbibliothek, kein Performance-Ranking.

| Eintrag | Plattformen | Warum / Anwendung | Status / Grenze |
|---|---|---|---|
| [googleads/google-ads-mcp](https://github.com/googleads/google-ads-mcp/blob/3362b43cb92f60afe4de3ffd382bcdd053ee5f4f/README.md) | google | Offizieller Datenzugang für GAQL-Abfragen aus dem Assistenten. Zuerst Kampagnen, Conversion-Aktionen und Suchbegriffe lesend abfragen; daraus eine priorisierte Änderungsliste erzeugen. | external_reference; Ein MCP liefert Zugriff, keine profitable Strategie; Schreibwerkzeuge und Kontorechte gesondert prüfen. |

- Reference catalog covering 18 advertising platforms, publisher/AdTech and cross-channel methods; not a claim of worldwide completeness.
- This public repository contains no live account adapters, customer account data, OAuth configuration, analysis snapshots or private assistant code.
- 100 earlier skill pins are retained. Additional skills are source-indexed; not every instruction has received a full functional or security audit.
- README review, file hashes and software checks do not establish real-account performance or advertising uplift.
- Third-party license notices remain in place. Three skills are source links only because a suitable redistribution license was not confirmed.
- YouTube search results are samples. New metadata is not a transcript review, time series or measurement of trend velocity.
- No automatic installation, paid provider invocation or advertising-account mutation.
